actions)\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 8 — AUDIT TRAIL\n\n### 8.1 Strategy\n\nAudit Trail là **immutable, append-only record** của mọi state change có significance trong hệ thống. Không xóa, không sửa. Chỉ append.\n\n### 8.2 Audit Entry Structure\n\n```\nAuditEntry:\n  id:            UUID (generated server-side)\n  timestamp:     ISO 8601 with milliseconds\n  actor_type:    'HUMAN' | 'AI_SYSTEM' | 'AUTOMATED' | 'API_EXTERNAL'\n  actor_id:      admin user ID | 'AI_ENGINE_v1' | 'CRON_JOB' | 'VNPAY_WEBHOOK'\n  actor_role:    role at time of action (snapshot — role may change)\n  actor_ip:      IPv4/IPv6 (for HUMAN actors)\n  actor_session: session ID (for HUMAN actors)\n\n  domain:        'tutor' | 'user' | 'service' | 'booking' |\n                 'finance' | 'moderation' | 'settings' | 'system'\n  entity_type:   'tutor_profile' | 'booking' | 'complaint' | 'transaction'...\n  entity_id:     the ID of the changed entity\n\n  action:        string (APPROVE_TUTOR | REJECT_TUTOR | SUSPEND_USER |\n                         RESOLVE_COMPLAINT | RELEASE_ESCROW | etc.)\n  action_label:  Vietnamese label for display\n\n  old_value:     JSON snapshot of entity before change (nullable)\n  new_value:     JSON snapshot of entity after change\n\n  metadata:      JSON — additional context\n                 (reason, notes, AI confidence, override rationale, etc.)\n  tags:          string[] (e.g., ['financial', 'high_value', 'ai_involved'])\n```\n\n### 8.3 Audit Coverage by Domain\n\n```\nDomain          Events logged\n────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nTutor Center    profile_status_changed, document_viewed, approval_email_sent\nUser Center     role_changed, suspended, banned, password_reset\nService Center  status_changed, price_overridden, content_removed\nBooking Center  status_changed, dispute_opened, dispute_resolved,\n                escrow_action (hold/release/refund)\nFinance Center  payout_approved, payout_rejected, refund_processed,\n                commission_changed, fraud_flagged\nModeration      complaint_assigned, ai_run, ai_overridden, complaint_resolved,\n                review_removed, violation_actioned, appeal_decided\nSettings        config_changed, feature_flag_toggled, admin_created,\n                role_assigned, ai_config_changed, gateway_configured\nSystem          login_attempt (failed), bulk_export, data_access_sensitive\n```\n\n### 8.4 Audit Access Rules\n\n```\nEntry type          Who can view\n────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nOwn actions         All admin roles (everyone sees what they did)\nDomain actions      Admins with access to that domain\nFinancial actions   finance_manager + admin + super_admin\nSettings actions    super_admin only\nSensitive docs      super_admin only (CCCD, certificates viewed)\nFull raw export     super_admin only\n```\n\n### 8.5 Audit Timeline UI (per entity)\n\nKhi xem bất kỳ entity nào (tutor, booking, complaint...) trong drawer, tab **Lịch sử** hiển thị:\n\n```\n[Timeline]\n│\n├── 2026-06-29 14:32:05  HUMAN (admin@edux.com)\n│   Action: APPROVE_TUTOR\n│   \"Đã duyệt hồ sơ gia sư. Ghi chú: Chứng chỉ hợp lệ, CCCD rõ ràng.\"\n│\n├── 2026-06-29 10:15:22  AI_SYSTEM (AI_ENGINE_v1)\n│   Action: AI_ASSESSED\n│   \"Đánh giá tự động: Hồ sơ đầy đủ, không cờ đỏ. Confidence: 82%.\"\n│\n├── 2026-06-27 09:00:00  HUMAN (tutor: T-001)\n│   Action: PROFILE_SUBMITTED\n│   \"Gia sư nộp hồ sơ lần đầu.\"\n│\n└── 2026-06-25 15:44:11  AUTOMATED (system)\n    Action: ACCOUNT_CREATED\n    \"Tài khoản được tạo qua Google OAuth.\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 9 — GLOBAL SEARCH\n\n### 9.1 Strategy\n\nGlobal Search là **unified, cross-entity search** với keyboard shortcut. Không phải per-domain search.\n\nTrigger: Header search box, hoặc `Ctrl+K` / `Cmd+K`.\n\n### 9.2 Search Scope\n\n```\nEntity Type       Searchable Fields                          Result Title\n──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nUser              name, email, phone, user ID               \"Student: Nguyễn Văn A\"\nTutor             name, email, subjects, tutor ID           \"Tutor: Trần Thị B\"\nService/Course    title, tutor name, subject, service ID    \"Course: Luyện thi Toán...\"\nBooking           booking ID, student name, tutor name      \"Booking BK-001\"\nTransaction       transaction ID, amount, gateway txn ID    \"Transaction TX-4421\"\nComplaint         case ID, title, reporter name             \"Complaint CASE-2026-001\"\nWithdrawal        withdrawal ID, tutor name, amount         \"Withdrawal WD-009\"\nPayout            same as withdrawal                        \"Payout PO-012\"\n```\n\n### 9.3 Search Result Structure\n\n```\n[Search Results for \"Nguyễn Văn A\"]\n\n┌─ USERS (2) ──────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  👤 Nguyễn Văn A — student — nguyenvana@gmail.com    │\n│     last login: 2 ngày trước · status: active        │\n│                                                       │\n│  👤 Nguyễn Văn Anh — parent — ...                    │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\n┌─ BOOKINGS (1) ────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  📅 BK-4421 — Nguyễn Văn A × Trần Thị B              │\n│     Toán · 2026-06-28 · Completed · 500,000 ₫        │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\n┌─ COMPLAINTS (1) ──────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  🛡️ CASE-2026-001 — Submitted by Nguyễn Văn A        │\n│     \"Gia sư đi trễ\" · OPEN · SLA: 18h còn lại        │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 9.4 Search Behaviors\n\n```\nInstant results:   Debounced 300ms, show top 3 per entity type\nFull results:      Press Enter → full results page /admin/search?q=...\nRecent searches:   Last 10 queries stored in localStorage\nKeyboard nav:      ↑/↓ to move, Enter to open, Esc to close\nPermission filter: Results filtered by caller's role\n                   (finance_manager cannot see suspension notes)\nDeep link:         Each result links directly to entity in its domain\nAction shortcut:   Results can show quick actions inline\n                   (e.g., Approve button next to pending tutor result)\n```\n\n### 9.5 Search Syntax Extensions (future)\n\n```\nPrefix search:\n  tutor:Nguyễn        → search only in tutors\n  booking:BK-         → search booking IDs\n  amount:>5000000     → transactions above 5M ₫\n  status:pending      → items in pending state\n  date:2026-06        → items from June 2026\n  assigned:me         → Operations Center items assigned to current admin\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 10 — NOTIFICATION CENTER\n\n### 10.1 Notification Types\n\n```\nType          Description                                    Admin Action?\n────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nALERT         Requires action (SLA breach, critical case)     YES\nTASK          New item assigned to admin                      YES\nWARNING       Approaching SLA deadline (<2h)                  YES\nINFO          Informational (batch job done, report ready)    NO\nSUCCESS       Action confirmed (payout approved, etc.)        NO\nSYSTEM        Platform alerts (downtime, error spike)         admin+ only\n```\n\n### 10.2 Notification Sources\n\n```\nSource              Trigger                               Type\n──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nTutor Center        New pending approval                  TASK\n                    Tutor responded to doc request        TASK\n                    Approval SLA >72h approaching         WARNING\nBooking Center      New dispute opened                    TASK\n                    Dispute SLA approaching               WARNING\nFinance Center      Payout request submitted              TASK\n                    Fraud alert triggered                 ALERT\n                    Payout failed after processing        ALERT\nModeration          Complaint AWAITING_ADMIN              TASK\n                    CRITICAL complaint                    ALERT\n                    SLA breach                            ALERT\n                    AI could not resolve (confidence <70%)TASK\nAnalytics           Scheduled report ready                INFO\nSystem              AI engine error                       SYSTEM\n                    Payment gateway down                  SYSTEM (ALERT)\n```\n\n### 10.3 Notification Routing\n\n```\nRouting Rules:\n  ALERT (any domain)     → super_admin + admin + relevant role\n  CRITICAL complaint     → super_admin + admin (regardless of assignment)\n  Payout task           → finance_manager + admin + super_admin\n  Content violation      → content_moderator + admin + super_admin\n  Tutor approval         → admin + super_admin\n  Complaint task         → support_staff + content_moderator + admin\n  SYSTEM alert          → super_admin + admin only\n  Assignment-specific   → only assigned admin\n\nDelivery channels:\n  In-app (header bell icon):  all types\n  Email digest (daily):       ALERT + TASK only, grouped by domain\n  Real-time push (future):    CRITICAL + ALERT only\n```\n\n### 10.4 Notification Structure\n\n```\nNotification:\n  id:             UUID\n  recipient_id:   admin user ID\n  type:           ALERT | TASK | WARNING | INFO | SUCCESS | SYSTEM\n  domain:         source domain\n  entity_type:    source entity type\n  entity_id:      source entity ID\n  title:          string (short: \"Khiếu nại CRITICAL mới\")\n  body:           string (detail: \"Lê Văn A báo cáo vi phạm nghiêm trọng...\")\n  action_label:   string (\"Xem ngay\")\n  action_url:     deep link to entity in domain\n  is_read:        boolean\n  created_at:     ISO timestamp\n  expires_at:     ISO timestamp (notifications auto-expire after 30 days)\n```\n\n### 10.5 Notification Center UI Design\n\n```\n[Header: Bell icon with unread badge]\n  → click → dropdown (latest 10, grouped)\n\n[Full Notification Center: /admin/notifications]\n  Tabs: All | Unread | Alerts | Tasks\n  Filters: Domain | Date | Type\n  Actions:\n    - Mark all read\n    - Clear read notifications\n    - Per-notification: mark read, dismiss, open entity\n  Group by domain (collapsible)\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 11 — AI CENTER (Embedded Architecture)\n\n### 11.1 Principle\n\nAI không phải là một domain sidebar. AI là capability layer embedded vào từng domain. Admin không \"đi vào AI\" — AI xuất hiện trong workflow tự nhiên của từng domain.\n\n### 11.2 AI Capabilities Map\n\n```\nDomain              AI Capability                     Status\n─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nTutor Center        Profile completeness scoring       ← design only\n                    Risk flagging (missing docs)       ← design only\nService Center      Content quality check              ← design only\n                    Plagiarism detection (future)      ← future\nBooking Center      No-show prediction (future)        ← future\nModeration          Complaint assessment + routing     ← IMPLEMENTED (mock LLM)\n                    Review sentiment analysis          ← design only\n                    Spam/fake review detection         ← design only\nDashboard           Platform health summary            ← partial (hardcoded)\nAnalytics           Anomaly detection                  ← design only\nMatching            Tutor-student matching score       ← IMPLEMENTED (rule-based)\n```\n\n### 11.3 AI Engine Architecture\n\n```\nINPUTS\n  complaint / content / profile / transaction\n       │\n       ▼\n[AGGREGATOR LAYER]\n  buildCaseContext(entity)\n  → fetchUserHistory(userId)\n  → fetchTutorMetrics(tutorId)\n  → fetchTransactionStatus(bookingId)\n  → fetchLessonContext(bookingId)\n       │ enrichedData\n       ▼\n[EVALUATION LAYER]\n  evaluateCase(enrichedData)\n  → HARD RULES CHECK (criticalKeywords → CRITICAL)\n  → LLM CALL (mockAIEngine → future: Gemini API)\n  → FRAUD PREVENTION (anti-abuse threshold)\n       │ aiResult: { severity, confidence, recommendation, actionCode, autoResolved }\n       ▼\n[ACTION LAYER]\n  executeAction(actionCode, caseId, actorType, actorId, updateCaseStatus)\n  → performAction(actionCode) → domain state change\n  → appendAuditLog(caseId, actorType, actorId, action, metadata)\n\nOUTPUTS\n  → Complaint status updated\n  → Audit entry created\n  → Notification sent if AWAITING_ADMIN\n  → Operations Center item created if needs human\n```\n\n### 11.4 AI Configuration (Settings > AI Config — super_admin only)\n\n```\nSettings:\n  LLM Provider:         Gemini | OpenAI | Mock (current)\n  Model:                gemini-1.5-pro | gpt-4o | ...\n  Confidence Threshold: 70 (below this → force manual review)\n  Critical Keywords:    [editable list]\n  SLA Targets:          CRITICAL:1h | HIGH:24h | MEDIUM:48h | LOW:72h\n  Anti-Abuse:           auto_resolve_threshold_30d: 3\n                        appeal_threshold_30d: 2\n  Matching Weights:     subject:35 | format:15 | budget:15 | city:10\n                        experience_max:15 | rating:5 | (sum=95+5=100)\n```\n\n### 11.5 AI Performance Monitoring (Analytics > AI Performance)\n\n```\nMetrics tracked:\n  Auto-resolution rate         (% complaints AI resolved without human)\n  Human override rate          (% AI decisions overturned by admin)\n  Confidence distribution      (histogram of confidence scores)\n  False positive rate          (auto-resolved complaints reopened)\n  SLA compliance by AI         (% AI-handled cases within SLA)\n  Matching conversion rate     (matches → bookings)\n  Fallback rate                (% matching requests used fallback tutors)\n  Average AI response time     (latency of LLM calls)\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 12 — DASHBOARD KPI\n\n### 12.1 KPI Architecture\n\nDashboard không hardcode. Mỗi KPI card là một **widget với data source** rõ ràng.\n\n### 12.2 KPI Cards — Row 1: Platform Health\n\n```\n┌─────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐\n│ Active Bookings │ │ Today Revenue   │ │ Pending Approvals│ │ Open Complaints │\n│ (today)         │ │                 │ │                  │ │ (needs action)  │\n│                 │ │                 │ │                  │ │                 │\n│ Source:         │ │ Source:         │ │ Source:          │ │ Source:         │\n│ /api/admin/     │ │ /api/admin/     │ │ /api/admin/      │ │ complaints table│\n│ bookings/stats  │ │ finance/today   │ │ tutors/stats     │ │ .status=AWAITING│\n│                 │ │                 │ │ .pending         │ │                 │\n│ Trend: vs. 7d   │ │ Trend: vs. 30d  │ │ SLA: oldest item │ │ CRITICAL badge  │\n└─────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 12.3 KPI Cards — Row 2: Operations\n\n```\n┌─────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐\n│ Overdue SLA     │ │ Matching Today  │ │ AI Auto-resolve │ │ Pending Payouts │\n│ Items           │ │ (requests)      │ │ Rate            │ │ (value)         │\n│                 │ │                 │ │                  │ │                 │\n│ Source:         │ │ Source:         │ │ Source:          │ │ Source:         │\n│ ops_center.     │ │ tutor_requests  │ │ complaints.      │ │ withdrawals.    │\n│ sla_status=     │ │ .created_today  │ │ closed_by_ai/    │ │ status=PENDING  │\n│ OVERDUE         │ │                 │ │ total            │ │                 │\n│                 │ │ vs. conversions │ │ 7-day average    │ │ count + amount  │\n└─────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 12.4 Charts\n\n```\nChart 1: Booking Volume (line, 30 days)\n  Data: bookings.created_at grouped by day\n  Y-axis: count | X-axis: date\n  Overlay: revenue line (secondary Y-axis)\n\nChart 2: Platform GMV vs. Commission (stacked bar, 6 months)\n  Data: transactions.amount grouped by month, split by type\n  Colors: GMV (blue) | Commission (green) | Refunds (red)\n\nChart 3: User Growth (area, 6 months)\n  Data: users.created_at grouped by month, split by role\n  Lines: Students | Tutors | Parents\n\nChart 4: Complaint Resolution Performance (donut)\n  Data: complaints grouped by resolution type\n  Segments: AI Resolved | Human Resolved | Pending | Overdue\n```\n\n### 12.5 AI Summary Widget\n\nReplace current hardcoded \"Tóm tắt AI nền tảng\" với:\n\n```\nAI PLATFORM SUMMARY\nData source: aggregated from real DB queries, not hardcoded\n\nLines:\n  - \"N hồ sơ gia sư đang chờ duyệt\" (real: tutors.status=pending count)\n  - \"N khiếu nại vượt SLA\" (real: complaints where now > sla_deadline)\n  - \"N giao dịch nghi ngờ gian lận\" (real: fraud_alerts.status=open)\n  - \"Tỷ lệ khớp AI tháng này: N%\" (real: matched+converted/total)\n  - \"N gia sư không hoạt động >30 ngày\" (real: tutors with no booking)\n\nRefresh: every 5 minutes (or manual)\nFallback: if API fails → show \"Đang cập nhật...\" not hardcoded stale data\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 13 — SHARED COMPONENTS\n\n### 13.1 Current State (Problem)\n\nShared components hiện đang nằm trong `admin/transactions/components.jsx` — sai vị trí, gây coupling. `Complaints.jsx` phải import từ transactions domain.\n\n### 13.2 Proposed Shared Component Library\n\nTất cả shared components chuyển về `admin/shared/` hoặc `admin/components/`:\n\n```\nadmin/\n  shared/\n    components/\n      DataTable.jsx          ← sortable, paginated, bulk-select, row actions\n      EntityDrawer.jsx       ← right-side slide panel, tabbed content\n      FilterChips.jsx        ← filter bar with chip toggles\n      StatCard.jsx           ← KPI card with icon, value, trend\n      StatusBadge.jsx        ← colored badge for entity status\n      StatusDot.jsx          ← small colored dot for inline status\n      SLABadge.jsx           ← ON_TRACK / DUE_SOON / OVERDUE badge\n      PriorityBadge.jsx      ← CRITICAL / HIGH / MEDIUM / LOW badge\n      ActionMenu.jsx         ← \"⋮ More\" dropdown anchored to button\n      BulkActionBar.jsx      ← floating bar when rows selected\n      ConfirmationModal.jsx  ← confirm dangerous action\n      Toast.jsx              ← temporary notification (already exists)\n      PageHeader.jsx         ← title + description + action buttons\n      Breadcrumb.jsx         ← navigation path display\n      EmptyState.jsx         ← zero-data illustration + message\n      LoadingSkeleton.jsx    ← placeholder while loading\n      AuditTimeline.jsx      ← chronological audit log display\n      SearchInput.jsx        ← search bar with clear + loading state\n      DateRangePicker.jsx    ← start/end date selection\n      DomainLink.jsx         ← cross-domain navigation link with icon\n      AIAssessmentCard.jsx   ← shows AI result: severity, confidence, recommendation\n      ModalOverlay.jsx       ← ESC + backdrop close (already exists)\n    hooks/\n      useAuditLog.js         ← fetch + display audit entries for entity\n      usePermission.js       ← check current user's permissions\n      useOperationsQueue.js  ← fetch ops center items\n      useToast.js            ← toast state management\n      useSearch.js           ← global search state\n    utils/\n      formatters.js          ← fmtVND, fmtDate, fmtCompactVND (centralized)\n      permissions.js         ← permission check functions\n      sla.js                 ← SLA calculation functions\n```\n\n### 13.3 Component Contracts\n\n```\nDataTable props:\n  columns: Column[]          ← { key, label, sortable, render }\n  data: any[]\n  loading: boolean\n  selectable: boolean        ← enables bulk selection\n  onSort: (key, dir) → void\n  onSelect: (ids: Set) → void\n  onRowClick: (row) → void\n  pagination: { page, pageSize, total, onChange }\n  emptyState: EmptyStateProps\n\nEntityDrawer props:\n  entity: any\n  show: boolean\n  tabs: Tab[]                ← { key, label, content: ReactNode }\n  activeTab: string\n  onTabChange: (key) → void\n  onClose: () → void\n  width: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg'  ← 400 | 560 | 720px\n\nDomainLink props:\n  domain: 'user' | 'tutor' | 'service' | 'booking' | 'finance' | 'moderation'\n  entityId: string\n  entityType: string\n  label: string\n  mode: 'navigate' | 'drawer'  ← navigate to domain | open in drawer overlay\n\nAuditTimeline props:\n  entityType: string\n  entityId: string\n  limit: number\n  showFilters: boolean\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 14 — WORKFLOW DIAGRAMS\n\n### 14.1 Tutor Onboarding & Approval\n\n```\nTUTOR                    SYSTEM                    ADMIN\n  │                        │                         │\n  ├─ Register ────────────►│                         │\n  │  (role: tutor)         │ creates user record     │\n  │                        │                         │\n  ├─ Fill TutorProfileForm►│                         │\n  │  (bio, subjects,       │ creates tutor_profile   │\n  │   cert upload,         │ status: PENDING         │\n  │   CCCD upload)         │                         │\n  │                        ├─── Notification ───────►│ TASK: \"New pending tutor\"\n  │                        │    TASK type            │ → Operations Center\n  │                        │                         │\n  │                        │                    ├── Open Operations Center\n  │                        │                    │    → click item\n  │                        │                    ├── Tutor Center → Pending list\n  │                        │                    │    → click tutor\n  │                        │                    │    → Drawer opens\n  │                        │                    ├── Review: bio, subjects, docs\n  │                        │                    │    → view cert (signed URL)\n  │                        │                    │    → view CCCD (signed URL)\n  │                        │                    │    → Audit: \"Document viewed\"\n  │                        │                    │\n  │                        │                    ├── [APPROVE]\n  │                        │◄── approve() ──────┤\n  │                        │                    │ Audit: APPROVE_TUTOR\n  │                        │ status→APPROVED    │\n  │                        │ wallet created     │\n  │◄─── Email: approved ───┤                    │ Toast: \"Đã duyệt ✓\"\n  │                        │                    │\n  │  OR                    │                    ├── [REQUEST MORE INFO]\n  │                        │◄── requestInfo() ──┤\n  │                        │ status→PENDING_INFO│ Audit: REQUEST_INFO\n  │◄─ Email: need docs ────┤                    │\n  │                        │                    │\n  │  OR                    │                    ├── [REJECT]\n  │                        │◄── reject(reason) ─┤\n  │                        │ status→REJECTED    │ Audit: REJECT_TUTOR\n  │◄─ Email: rejected ─────┤                    │ Toast: \"Đã từ chối\"\n```\n\n### 14.2 Student → AI Matching → Booking\n\n```\nSTUDENT                  SYSTEM                    ADMIN (passive)\n  │                        │                         │\n  ├─ /tutor-request ──────►│                         │\n  │  14-step wizard        │                         │\n  │                        │                         │\n  ├─ Submit form ─────────►│                         │\n  │                        │ POST /api/tutors/matches│\n  │                        │ scoring algorithm runs  │\n  │                        │ [!] persist request     │\n  │                        │ to tutor_requests table │\n  │                        │                         │\n  │◄─ Ranked tutors ───────┤                         │\n  │   (with match scores)  │ Analytics: request logged│ ← visible in Analytics\n  │                        │                         │   Matching tab\n  │                        │                         │\n  ├─ Compare tutors ──────►│                         │\n  │  (CompareModal)        │ track: compare event    │\n  │                        │                         │\n  ├─ Select tutor ────────►│                         │\n  │                        │ track: select event     │\n  │                        │                         │\n  ├─ /booking/:tutorId ───►│                         │\n  │  pick slot, confirm    │                         │\n  │                        │                         │\n  ├─ Pay ─────────────────►│ escrow HOLD             │\n  │  (VNPay / wallet)      │ booking created         │\n  │                        │ request.converted=true  │ ← conversion tracked\n  │                        │                         │\n  │                        ├─── Notification ───────►│ INFO: \"New booking BK-XXX\"\n  │                        │                         │ (not actionable, just info)\n  │                        │                         │\n  │◄─ Booking confirmed ───┤                         │\n```\n\n### 14.3 Complaint → AI → Resolution\n\n```\nUSER            AI ENGINE              ADMIN            FINANCE\n  │                 │                    │                 │\n  ├─ Submit ───────►│                    │                 │\n  │  complaint      │ buildCaseContext() │                 │\n  │                 │ ← fetch user hist  │                 │\n  │                 │ ← fetch tutor metr │                 │\n  │                 │ ← fetch escrow st  │                 │\n  │                 │ ← fetch lesson log │                 │\n  │                 │                    │                 │\n  │                 │ evaluateCase()     │                 │\n  │                 │ → hard rules check │                 │\n  │                 │ → LLM evaluation   │                 │\n  │                 │ → anti-abuse Sidebar navigates by URL.\n\nRule 2 — Secondary context via Drawer\n  Viewing related entity without leaving current domain → drawer overlay.\n  Drawer shows read-only summary + \"Xem đầy đủ\" link.\n\nRule 3 — Actions stay in source domain\n  Trigger a Finance action from Moderation? → API call in background.\n  Toast confirms. User stays in Moderation.\n\nRule 4 — Ops Center as universal re-entry point\n  Any item requiring admin action always surfaces in Ops Center.\n  Admin can start their day from Ops Center without touching sidebar.\n\nRule 5 — Filter propagation\n  Analytics → Domain: URL carries filter params.\n  Receiving domain shows \"Đang lọc theo: [filter]\" with clear option.\n\nRule 6 — Audit trail follows entity, not domain\n  Audit log accessible from every entity drawer.\n  Full audit log in Settings for super_admin.\n```\n\n### Non-Negotiable Data Fixes Before Implementation\n\n```\n1.
